- Profile: Settings › General › Profile stores prefs `userProfile` (`{name, experience}`; contracts and prompt text in dependency-free `resources/gui-bridge/modes/user-profile-contracts.ts`; levels beginner, vibe-coder, junior, expert). It rides the reply-language path: `OMP_GUI_USER_PROFILE` (JSON) at spawn, `user_profile_set` on change and on bridge connect to every live local sidecar and hosted tab; `modes/user-profile.ts` appends one `<user-profile>` section through `before_agent_start` (nothing for an empty profile). Stock has no such setting.
- Reply language: Settings › General › Conversation language stores prefs `replyLanguage` (`auto` default | a curated English name | `off`; `src/shared/reply-language.ts`; missing or unknown reads as `auto`). `auto` is the device language's English name (first usable `app.getPreferredSystemLanguages()` entry, then `app.getLocale()`; not the UI language). Each spawn passes it as `OMP_GUI_REPLY_LANGUAGE`; a pref change (or bridge connect) pushes it to every live local sidecar and every tab this machine hosts via the bridge's `reply_language_set {language: string | null}`. A paired host's tabs follow the host's own pref.
- `modes/reply-language.ts` keeps the value as module state and appends one instruction through `before_agent_start` from the next turn (nothing when off, unset or English, so the prompt stays stock); `currentReplyLanguage()` / `onReplyLanguageChange()` feed the session titles (see Automatic session titles).
- A second line in the same section, naming the same resolved language (never hardcoded), covers the labels a person sees: todo items and phase names, and a `Title: <short sentence>` first line in each `task` assignment. A subagent's stock title (`generateTaskLabel`, `task/label.ts`) is always English with no hook and stock's task tool has no `description` field, so the GUI shows that `Title:` line (`assignmentTitle` in `modes/subagent-title-contracts.ts`, applied in `subagentPrimaryLabel` for sidebar rows, the Subagent panel header and the work board, and in `stores/subagents.ts` / `work-run.ts` for the work map; no line → stock's label). Thinking, the rest of every assignment, tool arguments, code and file contents stay as the model writes them (Korean costs more tokens).
- Pinned by `bridge-reply-language.test.ts`, `subagent-graph.test.ts` and the opt-in `e2e/real-subagent-titles.e2e.ts` (Korean and Japanese). Never inject GUI text with `--append-system-prompt`: it collides with the launch profile's flag and turns off omp's fork prompt-cache key.

### Sidebar memory
- The left side keeps what was left open or closed across a relaunch, a reload and a reopened window. Device-local prefs `sidebarVisible`, `sidebarFolds`, `sidebarFamilies` (`DEVICE_LOCAL_PREF_KEYS`: a paired web page keeps them per device, the desktop per profile). Code: `lib/sidebar-memory.ts`, `stores/sidebar-folds.ts`, `stores/sidebar-subagents.ts`, `stores/ui.ts`. Each pref has a localStorage mirror (`omp.sidebarVisible`, `omp.sidebarFolds`, `omp.sidebarFamilies`) read synchronously by the first render; the pref is read again after mount and wins when it differs, unless the user already edited.
- **Shown/hidden**: `useUiStore.sidebarVisible` starts from the mirror and every change is saved by a `useUiStore` subscription, except while the shell is a drawer (phone, portrait tablet: `AppShell` `useDrawerSidebarSync` calls `setSidebarDrawerMode`; the flag is then the drawer's open state, never saved, and the drawer always starts closed; saving resumes when the window docks). `pre-paint.js` sets `data-boot-sidebar="hidden"` from `omp.sidebarVisible` and the boot shell (`scripts/boot-shell.ts`) drops its sidebar and clears the traffic lights, so the first frame never flashes an open sidebar.
- **Folds** (`sidebarFolds`: `groups` project / folder / worktree group `cwd` → folded, `devices` `local` and each paired host's deviceId, `deviceGroups` a host's folders as `deviceId\ncwd`, `lane` Code/Chat, `archivedOpen`, `starterOpen` Get started, `usageCollapsed` the footer usage block): the remembered lane beats the open session's lane at launch and the session wins from its next change on; a filter still shows every match.
- **Families** (`sidebarFamilies`: session paths whose family rows are open or hidden): a remembered open family keeps its entry until children have been seen and gone (`FamilyGroup`'s `hadChildren`), never at launch while they have not reported yet; a live tab's `tab:` key is never saved.
- **Bounded**: each record keeps its newest `FOLD_LIMIT` (50) entries and keys ≤ 200 characters (a paired device's whole `uiPrefs` record is capped at 64 KiB, `DEVICE_UI_PREFS_MAX_BYTES`: a write over it is refused for every pref in it). The sidebar prunes entries of folders, hosts and sessions that no longer exist (`pruneGroups`, `pruneDevices`, `pruneFamilies`) only once a live read answered (`useSessionList().live`, never the last-known snapshot of the newest 150 rows) and the host list has loaded.
- Tests: `sidebar-memory.test.ts`, `sidebar-folds.test.ts`, `ui-sidebar-visible.test.ts`, "Sidebar remembered folds" in `Sidebar.test.tsx`, `device-prefs.test.ts`; `e2e/sidebar-memory.e2e.ts` (collapse, fold two projects, relaunch, read the boot shell's and the app's first frame). A test that mounts the sidebar resets `useSidebarFolds` in `afterEach`.

## Composer

User's rules (19 Oct 2026): **composer controls never move outside the composer box, and the phone composer never folds**; **the Run sheet is one line per row**. Never undo either.

### Run controls (Stop, Esc, queue)

Code: `lib/messages.ts` (`abortActiveTurn`, `stopFromEscape`, `restoreAbortedQueue`, `resumeRestoredQueue`), `stores/composer.ts`. Stock's semantics, nothing new on the wire.

- **One Stop per turn**: `composer.stopping` is set before `abort_and_restore_queue` goes out and held until the session's `isStreaming` goes false (10 s cap, `STOP_SETTLE_MS`), so a double click, a double Esc or Stop + Esc send one command.
- **Esc while a turn runs and the field holds text or a picture only arms** (`composer.stopArmedAt`, `DOUBLE_ESC_MS` 1.5 s; an edit of the draft disarms; the view says "Press Esc again to stop", `input.escAgainToStop`). A second Esc inside the window, or an empty field, stops. Popovers/menus/dialogs that consume the Esc never abort (`defaultPrevented`/`[role=dialog]` guards in `App.tsx`). The Stop button never asks twice.
- **Stop never releases the queue**: stock withdraws it. `restoreAbortedQueue` keeps each message its own `composer.restoredQueue` item (steers, then follow-ups, oldest first) while a view that shows them is mounted (`ComposerRestoredQueue` registers `restoredTrayMounted`): Resume sends the first as a `followUp`-behaviour prompt and the rest as follow-ups, "Put in the field" edits one, nothing is sent by itself. Without such a view they merge ahead of the draft with a blank line between and one toast.
- **Withdrawing a prompt that waits on the automatic mode choice returns at once** (`runAutoMode` `signal`, `composer.decidingMode` for the "deciding" cue); a decision the host makes afterwards is ended when it lands (`onLateDecision`).
- **⇧Tab is never ours** (keymap chords need ⌃/⌥/⌘; Shift+Tab is focus navigation). The thinking level cycles on `thinking.cycle` (⌥⇧T on both platforms, remappable in Settings › Keyboard and the palette).
- **Pictures sent as steers** are remembered by queue chip id in the queue store (`expectImages`, matched by text when the chip appears, dropped when an edit or a steer→follow-up move re-queues text only), so the pill and popover count them although stock's lanes are text only. A picture-only chip's "[Image]" is the UI language's word (`lib/queue-chip.ts`). `queue.moved` says where an edited message landed (the end of its lane).
- `lib/composer-blocked.ts` `composerBlockedBy` names what the cards above wait for (`ask` | `approval` | `plan`). Placeholder priority: blocked > editing queued > suggestion > follow-up > steer > idle.
- **A session activation hands the next key to the composer** (`lib/tab-focus.ts`, installed in `App.tsx`): after a tab or session change, focus on `<body>`, a sidebar button or a pane that just left moves to the active composer; a dialog, a text field, the terminal, the files editor and touch input are left alone.
- The queue is one pill in the send cluster (`ComposerQueuePill`, `[data-queue-pill]`) opening a popover (a bottom sheet on a phone): lane headings (`queue-lane.tsx`), the policy line from `steeringMode`/`interruptMode`, rows `Edit: <text>` with Steer now primary. Nothing is added to the box while messages wait (`e2e/composer-queue-view.e2e.ts` checks 0 px). Edit queued (`composer.editingQueued`, `ComposerQueueEdit.tsx`) takes the field over; Cancel/Esc restore the exact draft snapshot; Send becomes a save ✓ (`composerQueue.editSave`). Stop pauses: `ComposerRestoredQueue` is one 36 px row (chips, Resume, Discard). `ComposerQueueLive` is the single polite status region.
- Pinned by `messages.test.ts`, `auto-mode-client.test.ts`, `queue.test.ts`, `tab-focus.test.ts`, `ComposerRestoredQueue.test.tsx`, `e2e/composer-flows.e2e.ts`, `e2e/steer-send.e2e.ts`.

### Delegation strength

- The Run chip's Delegation section (`layout/composer/DelegationField.tsx`, mounted by `RunChip.tsx`; the phone's Run sheet shows it too; hidden when the omp cannot report a strength) sets Your settings / Solo / Assisted / Collaborative / Swarm per tab through `get_delegation`/`set_delegation` → bridge `modes/delegation.ts` (rules `delegation-core.ts`, presets `delegation-contracts.ts`).
- Each strength is stock `task.eager`, `task.maxConcurrency` and (Solo) `task.maxRecursionDepth = 0` held as runtime overrides, never written to config.yml; stock's own listeners rebuild the prompt and drop or restore the task tool.
- The last pick is prefs `delegationMode`, passed as `OMP_GUI_DELEGATION` on every spawn (`sidecar.ts`; omitted for "default") and applied inside the extension factory like the thinking default. A Settings write of one of those settings releases the strength.

### Working cue

- `layout/composer/ComposerLive.tsx`, `composer-live.ts`, look in `styles/components.css`: a slow accent light travels the composer border while the main turn runs (`turn`); a fainter, slower one plus a ringed Send with a hint tooltip shows while the turn is idle but this tab's subagents or background shells still run (`background`). Both read the stores the work board and dock read (no polling), fade to a faint trace while the field holds a draft (Send ring dropped; `.omp-composer-box[data-composer-draft]`), never show beside a waiting question or approval, rest while the window is hidden, and animate `transform`/`opacity` only.
- Reduced motion and **software compositing** get the still form: a 1 px line on the box's top edge at 40 % (25 % for `background` or with a draft) and a still Send ring. Main sets `html[data-gpu="software"]` after `app.getGPUInfo("complete")` reports `gpu_compositing` not enabled (`softwareCompositing()` in `gpu-fallback.ts`; `getGPUFeatureStatus()` read earlier says software even on a healthy GPU). Any new looping cue draws still under it.
- Running-state light (`components/common/LiveEdge.tsx`, look in `styles/components.css` `.omp-live-edge`; 19 Oct 2026; supersedes the sidebar's old left-edge bar, its travelling light and the running pulse dot of "Sidebar families"): **a session that works wears the composer's light on its card**, one component and one stylesheet for every surface, never copied. `ComposerLiveEdge` (the composer box) and every card that shows a running session mount `<LiveEdge>`: a sidebar session row (`ListRow live`, `Sidebar.tsx` `signal === "running"`; also a host's row in `SidebarDevices.tsx`), and a split pane's header (`SplitPaneHeader`: `status` `running` without `awaitingInput`; compacting and (re)starting keep the spinner; `data-pane-running`). The old tab strip is gone, and the tab overview and switcher are lists, not cards, so nothing there. A phone's drawer is the same `Sidebar`. **Restraint (Main's pass, 19 Oct 2026: a family read as a stack of boxes)**: only top-level session rows and pane headers wear the light; a child row (subagent or opened session) stays a plain row whose status token is a still accent dot (waiting ⚠). The family chip shows its count once: while something runs, one filled still accent pill with the kind glyph(s) and the running count (`data-family-running`); with none running, the plain muted counts per kind (`data-family-agents`, `data-family-sessions`); the label keeps the totals. The only things that move are the lights. **Two forms of one light**: the composer box (one per window) is a 1 px masked ring with a conic gradient turning inside it (8 s, `omp-live-edge-turn`); a contained card (`data-live-edge-inside`, `inside` prop; a dozen can be in view) is a still 1 px accent line (30 % accent) with two 30 %-wide gradient bars, one on the top edge and one on the bottom edge, running opposite ways (8 s, `omp-live-edge-run`, one `transform: translateX`), so the light circles it clockwise. **Why two forms (measured, 1100x800 window, fake omp, three rows running, mean of 4 s, three runs; `e2e/sidebar-edge-light.e2e.ts`)**: the old left-edge bar 0.3–0.6 % GPU; the masked + clipped conic ring per row 1.3–1.7 % GPU (the same for a 100 % or 115 % square, a sliding gradient, with or without `will-change`; without the mask 1.1 %; mask, clip and the opacity group together are the cost, not the layer's size); two unmasked, unclipped bars per row 0.3 % GPU / 0.2–0.3 % renderer, the same as the old bar. So a card has no `opacity` group, no mask and no clip (`opacity: 1`, `overflow: visible`; pinned by `LiveEdge.test.tsx`). Streaming flow (`e2e/flow-performance.e2e.ts` `grid`, four panes streaming, a 1.4 MB clone, three runs alternating, before = v0.38.x with the bar): stream output GPU 58.0 → 60.0 %, renderer 74.9 → 76.9 %; stream agents GPU 46.6 → 47.9 %, renderer 42.4 → 44.1 % (the spread of the before alone is 9.5 points of GPU), so four rows, four headers and four composers added no visible cost. **Rules**: only visible running cards animate (a row out of view is skipped by its `content-visibility: auto`, a collapsed group renders none), `data-paused` rests every light while the window is hidden (`useWindowVisible`), waiting (ask/approval) keeps its warning bar and never wears the light, error keeps its danger bar. Reduced motion and **software compositing** draw the still form: the composer a 1 px line on its top edge at 40 %, a card its 1 px edge all round at 45 % with no bars (a line on a row's top alone would read as a separator). Nothing breathes by opacity (Apple HIG Motion: avoid sustained oscillation near 0.2 Hz). Design record: research notes (Apple HIG Motion, Vercel AI Elements Shimmer, Claude Code's thinking glyph, Linear's calmer sidebar; every source marked read or inferred) and three directions prototyped on the dev harness with the real components (light/dark, desktop and phone), scored 0–3 on calm, legible at a glance, cost, consistency with the composer, reduced-motion form: (a) the composer's light on the card border 15; (b) a still row with a spinner arc and a shimmering title 7 (two moving things per row, and a text shimmer repaints the text each frame, so it is no transform); (c) a still row with the count chip as a progress ring 11 (its fill would be a made-up fraction: a turn has no known length). Tests: `LiveEdge.test.tsx` (attributes, hidden-window rest, the CSS rules: transform-only, no mask/clip on a card, still forms), `Sidebar.test.tsx` and `SidebarSubagents.test.tsx` (running rows and children wear it, waiting/error/idle do not), `SplitWorkspace.test.tsx` (header), `ComposerLive.test.tsx`; e2e `row-glow.e2e.ts` (fake omp, three running tabs, a split, reduced motion), `row-glow-harness.e2e.ts` (dev harness, desktop and an iPhone 15 drawer, light/dark, `OMP_GUI_ROW_GLOW=<dir>` captures), `background-tabs.e2e.ts`, `composer-live.e2e.ts`, and the perf-gated `sidebar-edge-light.e2e.ts`. A new surface that shows a running session calls `<LiveEdge inside />` and nothing of its own.

### Modules

`layout/composer/`; `InputArea.tsx` only wires state and lays out; one concern per file.

- Views: `ComposerToolbar` (memoised: scalar props, stable handlers via `use-stable-callback.ts`, so typing does not re-render it), `RunChip` (+ `RunSettings.tsx`), `ComposerSheet`, `ComposerQueue.tsx`, `ComposerQueueEdit`, `queue-lane.tsx`, `ComposerRestoredQueue`, `ComposerUnderField`, `ComposerField`, `ComposerAttachments`, `CompletionList`, `PasteMenu`, `ComposerPickers`, `ComposerExpandedHeader`.
- Hooks `use-composer-*` (`field` owns the one `textareaRef`, `placeDraft`/`ownEdit`, `field-height.ts`; `keys`, `menu`, `commands`, `paste`, `attach`, `dictation`, `draft`, `history`, `events` for `omp:insert-mention`/`omp:fill-composer`, `suggestions`, `submit`).
- Helpers: `listbox-nav.ts`, `strip-roving.ts`, `paste-marker-edit.ts`, `keep-focus.ts`, `common/popover-focus.ts`, `lib/composer-actions.ts`, `lib/composer-blocked.ts`, `lib/tab-focus.ts`, `lib/hardware-keyboard.ts`.

### Toolbar (desktop/tablet)

- `RunChip.tsx` is the one trigger and popover for Mode (Plan/Goal/Autopilot/Auto mode, `ModesSection` of `ComposerModes.tsx`), Thinking, Tool approval, Delegation, Fast/Advanced/Model roles. `data-run-settings-trigger` is its hook; `data-active` paints it accent (never a utility-class tie, unlayered CSS: a `.bg-transparent` once beat the accent wash); the aria-label spells the whole state. "Mode popover" elsewhere means the Mode section of this popover.
- The model chip reads `Model · Thinking` (`data-model-chip`); the model picker has the thinking row.
- Steer/Stop/Send are reserved `.omp-composer-slot`s (`data-off` = inert and hidden, never unmounted: nothing moves when a turn starts, a draft appears or a mode changes). Labels fold by the toolbar's own `omp-toolbar` container (860/740/620/500 px), not the viewport.
- Below a 440 px toolbar (a pane split in four, 200 % zoom) the reserved slots that are off give their room back (`display: none`), because three of them cost Send its place (`e2e/desktop.e2e.ts` "narrow windows and 200 percent zoom"); only there does the row move when a turn starts.
- Popovers close on Tab (`common/popover-focus.ts`); the context ring opens on click/Enter only. The palette's History/Expand/Attach go through `omp:composer-action` (`lib/composer-actions.ts`).

### Phone composer

- The box is always open: a full-width field row over ONE compact controls row inside the box, the tablet's toolbar at phone size: `+`, the Run chip (a glyph: the mode's icon, the mode's name while one is on, a warning pip while tool approval is full access; its sheet is the Run sheet below), the mode extras, its own Thinking and Delegation chips (`PhoneRunChips.tsx`: Brain + the level's short name as a tiny badge, Network + how many subagents run at a time; the full value is the accessible name; each opens its field in a `ComposerSheet`), the model chip (`ProviderMark` and a name that ellipsizes inside its own 3.5 em box; the mark alone under 340 px of toolbar and while a turn runs), the context chip (`ContextUsagePopover variant="chip"`, `34 %`, breakdown in a `ComposerSheet`), Stop/Send|mic.
- Labels fold to icons by the toolbar's own width (`omp-toolbar`). Everything before Stop/Send is a sideways scroller (`omp-composer-scroll`, phone-only wrapper in `ComposerToolbar`; 8 px of padding taken back by the margin keeps the 44 px reach; chips sit 4 px apart with `--hit-gap-x: 4px` so no chip's reach covers a neighbour). Stop/Send/the queue pill sit outside it at the row's end, never scrolling or moving. No ⋯ toggle, status row or settings sheet outside the box exists (a ⋯ fold toggle and a 44 px status chip row above the box were both rejected by the user).
- **Nothing runs under Stop/Send** (a Galaxy, 390 px, ko once overflowed 67 px): idle at 390 and 412 the whole row fits with no scroll (the scroller ends 4 px before Send); running (Stop and an icon-only lane chip appear) it fits at 390 with the model as its mark; at 360 the model is its mark alone and a running row may scroll, clipped before Stop. `e2e/phone-run-sheet.e2e.ts` "keeps clear of Stop and Send" (360/390/412, en/ko): scroller ends before Stop/Send, model chip ≤ 130 px with an ellipsis, every control keeps ≥ 90 % of its taps.
- The `+` sheet carries attach/photos/history/expand/goal/skills only.
- Controls keep the field focused with `onMouseDown` `preventDefault` only, never `pointerdown` (it swallows the click in WebKit; `keep-focus.ts`).
- The field spans the box's inner width at every width: a 30-character Korean sentence stays on one line at 390 (`e2e/mobile-composer.e2e.ts` "wraps a long Korean line only at the box's inner width"; the old one-row grid wrapped Korean at half the box on a Galaxy S25 Ultra). It is capped at `min(40 % of the pane, visible − 120 px)` (`field-height.ts`) and refits on pane/visualViewport height changes. Touch sets `--omp-scrollbar-size: 0`.
- Idle chrome budget: `e2e/responsive-smoke.e2e.ts` `CHROME_SHARE_MAX` 19.4 % (measured 18.4 % at 390x844, plus one; the user chose the open composer over a lower share).
- First-paint shell follows the phone composer: `COMPOSER_BOX_PX.phone` 77 px (`src/shared/boot-shell-geometry.ts`) and `--omp-scrollbar-size: 0` under `(pointer: coarse)` in `scripts/boot-shell.ts`; regenerate `web.html` with `bun run gen:icons`; `e2e/no-loading.e2e.ts` compares the boot box with the React box within 2 px.

### Run sheet (one line per row)

User's rule, from phone screenshots (descriptions under every row, a full-width select with a caption, loose paragraphs, 2-line radio rows). The desktop/tablet popover is the same content at `w-96`.

- Every row of the sheet, the Mode section and Advanced is a lead glyph and its control on ONE line; every explanation sits behind an (i) glyph (`SettingInfo`, text kept in the DOM): no `data-setting-desc`, no `<p>`, no footer line.
- Switches are **tiles** (`common/Switch.tsx` `SwitchTile`: lucide glyph, a short label that truncates, (i), the switch; the whole tile toggles), two to a row in a `data-switch-grid` (`grid-cols-2`): Fast | Slow, Ultrafast (`SpeedFields`), then in Advanced (`AdvancedAndRoles`, opens by itself while a value is not the default) Auto-compact | Extended, Auto-retry | Prewalk, Batch steers | Interrupt. Glyphs: Zap, Turtle, Gauge, Shrink, Maximize2, RotateCw, Rocket, Layers, OctagonX. Fast's "not on this model" is a dimmed glyph plus a sentence in the (i) text. Tile labels ("Auto-compact", "자동 압축") are the old sentences' short forms; the sentences are the (i) texts (`input.more.*Desc`).
- Choices are **segmented rows** (`ChoiceRow.tsx`: the group's glyph, a radio row of short labels, (i) holding the group's name and the held option's effect; the full name is each segment's accessible name and tooltip; arrows move focus, `selectOnArrow` also picks): thinking level (`ThinkingLevelField`, `input.thinking.short.*`, variant `row`; the model picker keeps `segmented`), tool approval (`ApprovalModeField`, shield glyphs, `input.approval.short.*`; full access still asks first through the host's dialog), delegation (`DelegationField`, `input.delegation.short.*`; its "applies from the next turn" note is in the (i)). Short labels abbreviate the long names (ko/en parity).
- The compaction point is one line with its value in the label ("압축 시점 · 850k", `ContextSizeField`, the only select left, compact at the right, its other-layer note in the (i)).
- `mobile.css` bounds the reaches (`[data-choice-row]`, `[data-switch-grid]`: `--hit-gap` 4 px) and gives a segment and a tile the touch row height.
- Tests: `RunChip.sheet.test.tsx` (no visible description or paragraph, an (i) per explanation, tiles two to a row with glyphs, three segmented rows, one select), `ThinkingControl`/`ApprovalControl`/`DelegationField`/`ContextSizeField` tests, `ComposerToolbar.phone.test` (icon-first chips), `e2e/phone-run-sheet.e2e.ts` (`OMP_GUI_RUN_SHEET=<dir>` writes captures at 390 light/dark × en/ko; at 360/390/412 the sheet has no overflow, every tile label stays on one line, tiles pair up, a segmented row is ≤ 56 px, every switch and segment keeps ≥ 85 % of its own taps; the desktop popover keeps its width and cuts no label).

### Input mechanics

- The field is uncontrolled while typing (React 19 re-syncs a controlled textarea's `defaultValue` on every key, which cut ⌘Z to one character per press): the draft store is the source of truth and every composer write goes through `placeDraft`.
- A `[Paste #N, +L lines]` marker is atomic: Backspace/Delete/word/line deletes that touch it remove marker and blob as one step (`paste-marker-edit.ts`); a dangling `[Paste #N` never reaches the agent.
- `@` lists up to 20 000 files (`MENTION_FS_MAX_ENTRIES`) at depth 16 (`MENTION_FS_DEPTH`, `input-area-utils.ts`) ranked by `pathMatcher` (file name, word starts, CamelCase, Hangul initials); ←/→ keep the menu while inside the token (`arrowKeepsMenu`). The completion menu and the history overlay share `listbox-nav.ts`; the field is `role=combobox` while a menu exists.
- Attachment strips (`strip-roving.ts`): one tab stop each, arrows, Delete/Backspace, Backspace on an empty field focuses the last attachment, "Remove all" from 2.
- The draft flushes on `visibilitychange`/`pagehide` and on unmount.
- A draft past 8 000 characters (`LONG_DRAFT_CHARS`, `ComposerField.tsx`) is set in the system face (`[data-long]`): Inter Variable was reshaped for the whole text on every key.
- `compositionend` followed within 40 ms by Enter is ignored (iOS Safari's order, `POST_COMPOSITION_MS`, `lib/ime.ts`).

### Dictation and steer box

- The take shows as a bar under the field (`ComposerUnderField`, `DictationOverlay.tsx`, `use-composer-dictation.ts`, `lib/voice.ts`), never over the draft. A failed take keeps a `failure` kind (denied, no-device, in-use, busy, empty, no-model, failed): one sentence plus a Details toggle (none for busy/empty); only no-model offers Open Settings.
- The + menu lists "Dictation" while `stt.enabled` is off and opens Settings (`agent:setting-stt.enabled`) without writing it.
- The subagent steer box (`SteerSubagentForm`) grows like the main field (`fitFieldHeight`) and is never `disabled` while sending (`aria-busy`).

### Enter on a touch input

- `useShellInput() === "touch"` (desktop byte for byte unchanged): a soft keyboard's return key adds a line and only the Send button (or ⌘↵ / the follow-up chord) sends; `enterkeyhint="enter"` on the field.
- A physical keyboard on a tablet gets the desktop's Enter back: `lib/hardware-keyboard.ts` latches, for the page's life, the first key only such a keyboard makes (Esc, Tab, arrows, Home/End/Page keys, F-keys, a chord with ⌘ ⌃ ⌥, Shift with a named key such as Shift+Enter; never a letter, Backspace or Enter, which some soft keyboards report with a `code`).
- Settings › Appearance & input › "Enter sends on touch keyboards" (`displayPreferences.mobileEnterSends`, per device, default off) makes touch Enter send.
- The IME guard runs first, so a composition's Enter is never a send or a newline rule. In `!`/`$` shell drafts the field turns autocorrect, autocapitalize and spellcheck off.
- Pinned by `lib/hardware-keyboard.test.ts`, `InputArea.touch-enter.test.tsx`, `e2e/mobile-enter.e2e.ts` (Chromium + WebKit, en/ko).

### Design record (19 Oct 2026)

Decided by prototype (three variants each, scored on taps, visible state, density, layout jumps, touch/a11y, ko + narrowest width, stock semantics kept):

- **Toolbar → one Run chip with reserved slots and a toolbar-owned container** (19/21 vs 14 and 16): 6 controls instead of 8, Thinking in 2 taps instead of 3, nothing moves when a turn starts (the old row wrapped to 70 px at 340 px), Plan mode moves nothing to its left.
- **Queue → pill and popover** (15/21, the only variant winning the two heaviest criteria): the transcript moves 0 px when items queue (tray 75/83 px, in-box 40/44 px); price is 2-3 taps for lane switching and editing.
- The prototyped phone status chip row is **superseded by the user's rule** (controls stay in the box, the phone composer never folds); the status row and ⋯ sheet are gone.
- Not decided by prototype: undo granularity, atomic paste markers, `@` ranking, keyboard route to attachments, paste menu anchoring, strip cap, drop target, focus after a tab switch, draft flush on page hide.

### Tests

- E2E: `e2e/composer-toolbar.e2e.ts`, `composer-queue-view.e2e.ts`, `mobile-composer.e2e.ts`, `mobile-enter.e2e.ts`, `phone-run-sheet.e2e.ts`.
- Component: `InputArea.queue-run`, `InputArea.touch-enter`, `ComposerToolbar.test` / `ComposerToolbar.phone.test`, `RunChip.sheet.test`, `ComposerQueue.test`, `ComposerRestoredQueue.test`, `field-height.test`, `use-completion-menu.menu.test`.

## Smart features

All three bridge features (prompt suggestions, automatic titles, automatic mode) live in `resources/gui-bridge/modes/`, each as dependency-free `*-contracts.ts` + rules in `*-core.ts` + behaviour in `*-engine.ts`/`*.ts`, each with a capability flag. They never use the session model silently and never fall back to the main model; a missing model, credentials, refusal or offline state is a quiet `unavailable`/none, never a toast.

### Prompt suggestions
- Built in, Settings › Agent behavior. Prefs `nextPromptSuggestions` (default **on**) and sub-switch `nextPromptInline` (default on). Files: `next-prompt-contracts.ts`, `next-prompt-core.ts`, `next-prompt-engine.ts` behind `next-prompt.ts`.
- Plumbing = the reply language: `OMP_GUI_NEXT_PROMPT` JSON at spawn, `next_prompt_set` on change and on bridge connect to every live local sidecar and hosted tab; a paired host's tab follows the host's pref.
- Model: stock's `ComposerPredictionController` runs only in its TUI and `runEphemeralTurn` can only use the session model, so the engine calls stock `completeSimple` on the model stock's `smol` role resolves to (`resolveRoleChain` over `roleCandidatePool`, credentials via `modelRegistry.resolver`). Reasoning off, ≤ 512 output tokens, one retry without the cap when a transport refuses it. NEVER the main model. No model / no credentials / refused / offline → `unavailable` frames, shown as a muted `role=status` line under the composer plus a bridge debug log, never a toast.
- When: one request per finished turn (`agent_end`). Regenerated (debounced `SUGGEST_DEBOUNCE_MS` 800 ms, gated by `gateSuggestion`: enabled, idle, main agent, empty composer, a reply exists, state key moved) after a compaction, a finished user `!` command (renderer `refresh_next_prompt`), a session switch and an emptied draft. Typing (`set_next_prompt_draft`, from `use-idle-compaction-draft.ts`), a new run or the switch going off aborts the request; an answer whose moment moved meanwhile (new messages, work started/ended) is dropped and asked again.
- **Running work does NOT suppress suggestions** (a delegating session has jobs running at almost every turn end, so suppression would switch the feature off where it is used most); the model is told the work is underway and suggests waiting for, checking or steering it, or something unrelated.
- **Nothing to approve** (reported: after the assistant steered a session it opened and said so, the suggestion was "좋아 그 방향으로 가"; on a replay of that moment 2 of 7 runs showed such a line, 0 of 7 after the fix, `src/main/bridge-next-prompt-real.test.ts`, opt-in `OMP_GUI_REAL_WORK=1` with `NEXT_PROMPT_BEFORE_REF=<ref>`):
  - Sessions the main agent opened with the control tools run in their own tabs, so stock's jobs never list them. Main's `AgentControlRuntime` sends the opener's tab `set_next_prompt_children` (→ bridge `next_prompt_children`, a free command) with the running or waiting items of the work board's review list each time that list is pushed (only on a change, at most `MAX_NEXT_PROMPT_CHILDREN` 20); `<app-state>` shows them as `sessions-working-now` / `sessions-waiting-for-an-answer`.
  - `awaitsDecision` (in code, no model): an `ask` still open, or the reply's final paragraph ends in a question or puts a choice (`할까요`, `골라 주세요`, `should I`, `which do you prefer` …). `<app-state>` says `last-reply: asks the user to decide …` or `… reports work done or underway; it asks the user nothing`, and the rules allow approving only in the first case.
  - With no decision pending the cleaner drops a line made only of approval words (`isApprovalOnly`, `APPROVAL_WORDS`: "좋아 그 방향으로 가", "그렇게 해", "진행해", "ㅇㅋ 고", "go ahead", "sounds good, proceed"); any line naming something ("진행 상황 알려줘") stays.
  - A `write` to `xd://…` (a tool device) is not a changed file in `<app-state>`.
- Prompt content:
  - First `<user-style>` with ONE line only: `register: 반말 …` / `register: 존댓말 …` when most of the user's last 6 typed messages (`REGISTER_MESSAGES`) end in one Korean register (`userRegister` over the first lines of `sentTexts`, which leaves out stock's `synthetic` and `attribution: "agent"` user messages, slash commands and `<…>` blocks; `koreanRegister` majority, a tie names none; `koreanRegisterOf`: polite = ends in 요/니다/니까/죠 except nouns like 필요, casual = a listed 해체/해라체/음슴체 last syllable such as 줘, 해, 래, 지, 음, 임, anything else neutral).
  - **NEVER include excerpts of the user's messages**: given six excerpts the small model re-asked them as new requests (v0.32.2: 9 of 16 restated with six, 0 of 16 with none, register held).
  - Then the transcript tail (the reply being followed up clipped to 1800 chars, older ones to 700) plus a compact `<app-state>` read from the live session (last failed tool/command and exit, files changed this turn, open plan/todos, a question answered this turn, the main agent's running subagents and background jobs as `running-now` via stock `getAsyncJobSnapshot` — a subagent with the intent of the spawning `task` call —, goal, whether tests ran/failed, the user's `!` command, compaction).
  - Rules: stock `prompts/system/composer-prediction-user.md` voice/follow-up lines (write as the user), exactly 3 different next steps (asked for "up to 3" the cheap model often wrote one, so no chips showed), each a message the user sends to the assistant (never the assistant's voice, never addressed to the user, never offering/promising work), in the user's own voice, never repeat/reword/re-ask what they sent, use the `<user-style>` register, 3–8 words within the limits below, nothing already done, started, planned or handed to a subagent.
- **The register is enforced in code** (v0.32.1 voice rules alone still produced 존댓말 for a user who writes "배포해줘"). `cleanCandidateLine` / `cleanCandidates`, whatever the model writes:
  - a line in the assistant's voice (`isAssistantVoice`, conservative: ko `드릴게요/드릴까요/드리겠습니다`, `둘게요/두겠습니다`, `보신다면/하신다면/원하시면`, `알려 주시면`; en a leading `I'll`, `I will`, `Shall I`, `Would you like`, `Do you want me to`, `Let me` but not `Let me know`/`see`) is dropped whole;
  - `matchRegister`: for a casual user each sentence's polite end becomes its 반말 form by a fixed list (주세요 → 줘, 되나요 → 돼, 죠 → 지, and 요 after a 해체 syllable: 해요, 줄래요, 할까요, 있나요, 좋네요 …); any other polite end (니다, 세요, 예요) or a remaining honorific/humble word (주시, 하세, 드리, 제가, 저희 …) drops the line. For a polite user only 반말 ends a noun never has gain their polite form (줘 → 주세요, 돼 → 돼요, ㄹ래/ㄹ까 → +요, 지? → 죠?) and nothing is dropped: **a polite user is never pushed to 반말**. No register named → the line is left alone;
  - length: over `MAX_CANDIDATE_CHARS_CJK` 40 (any Hangul, Han or kana) / `MAX_CANDIDATE_CHARS` 60 keeps its first sentence when that fits (`fitCandidateLength`), else is dropped — never cut at a comma/word (a cut sends a different, unfinished request);
  - mojibake lines are dropped (see Mojibake from the model);
  - last, a line restating a message the user already sent is dropped (`SentMessages.isCopy` over every typed message of the session): F1 of a weighted longest common subsequence of units (each Hangul/Han/kana char, each Latin/digit run; a text's first 400), weighted by rarity across the sent messages (IDF), so shared rare words count, shared endings/everyday words hardly do, and Korean rewording still matches. `COPY_SIMILARITY` 0.6 against the latest message at any length, and against ANY sent message only from `COPY_MIN_UNITS` 14 units on (short generic requests recur and stay worth suggesting).
- Display: the best of up to 3 ranked one-line candidates is the placeholder (Tab/→); the rest are chips (↓ from an empty composer enters them, ←/→, Enter/click fills; Alt+1..3 fills directly; up to `min(100%, 44em)` wide so a 40-char Korean candidate shows whole, wrapping to a second row, full text as tooltip). Per-session memory in `stores/next-prompt.ts`: submit or a run clears it, a deleted draft brings it back.
- **Inline completion** (`complete_inline` → `next_prompt_inline`, same cheap model, ≤ 512 tokens, `InlineScheduler`): ghost text after the caret for a single-line draft of ≥ `INLINE_MIN_DRAFT_CHARS` 8 with the caret at its end, `INLINE_DEBOUNCE_MS` 600 ms after the last keystroke, at most one request start per `INLINE_MIN_INTERVAL_MS` 2 s (≤ 30 a minute, answers cached per draft; `e2e/next-prompt.e2e.ts` scripts 10 typing bursts in a minute and sees 10 requests). Aborted on every keystroke; never during IME composition, with a slash/`@` menu or paste choice open, for drafts starting `/`, `!`, `$`, `->`/`=>`, or a draft stock's `shouldSkipHistory` calls secret. Tab accepts, Esc dismisses.
  - Request carries the same register line (no excerpts); rules ask for the whole message, the draft restated character for character then continued on its own topic, one piece of the same sentence, never the assistant's words.
  - `cleanInlineReply` takes what follows the restated draft (`inlineDraftWindow`; trailing spaces of a draft ending in one are not doubled). A reply that does not start with the draft as typed shows nothing: **the user's text is never rewritten**. The ghost must be ≤ `INLINE_MAX_CHARS_CJK` 20 / `INLINE_MAX_CHARS` 50 (longer shows nothing, never cut), no role label, tag, fence or second sentence. Then the assembled message (draft + ghost) is judged — no assistant voice, no leaving the register (an opposite ending or, for a casual user, an honorific/humble word), no restatement of a sent message; each check counts only what the ghost brought. (Why the whole message is restated: asked for the continuation alone, 9 of 12 glued onto the last word.)
  - Debug log `next-prompt inline` records model, time, whether a ghost showed and the register, NEVER the text. `next_prompt_state.requests` counts both kinds.
- The user's own `next-prompt` extension is found through stock's loaded-extension list (`legacyExtension`); while the built-in is on the renderer ignores its widget; with the built-in off or unsupported the widget path works as before.
- Tests: `src/main/bridge-next-prompt-stock.test.ts` (smol resolution, legacy detection, reply cleaning vs stock's `parseComposerPrediction`), `bridge-next-prompt-core.test.ts`, `bridge-next-prompt.test.ts`; opt-in `OMP_GUI_REAL_WORK=1` `e2e/real-next-prompt.e2e.ts`. Loaders `config/model-roles`, `config/model-resolver`, `modes/controllers/input-controller`, `pi-ai` in `capabilities.ts` / `health.ts`.
- **Suggestions on touch** (`useShellInput() === "touch"`; desktop unchanged):
  - No Tab/→/↓/Alt+1..3/Esc, so `use-composer-suggestions.ts` builds the chips = best candidate FIRST, then the runner-ups (≤ `MAX_NEXT_PROMPT_CHIPS` 3), drawn by `NextPromptChips` as **one sideways-scrolling row** (`.omp-next-chips[data-touch]` in `mobile.css`: `nowrap`, `overflow-x: auto`, 32 px high, 28 px chips, scrollbar hidden, `--hit-gap-y: 4px` because an overflow container clips the global 44 px reach). The row sits just above the composer box (never inside). Nothing with no suggestion; hidden while typing, streaming or with a menu open.
  - The best candidate is **never also the placeholder** (`placeholderPrompt` undefined on touch; the placeholder stays the idle hint); no `Kbd` hints, `aria-keyshortcuts`, "to accept" chip or tooltip titles.
  - A **tap** fills the field, caret at the end, NOT sent (`pickChip` → `placeDraft`). A **long press** (the shell turns it into `contextmenu`) arms the chip and shows a "Send now" / "바로 보내기" button (`input.chips.sendNow`) that sends it through `send(text)`; any other pointer-down disarms.
  - **Inline ghost completion is OFF on touch** (`useInlineCompletion` gets `enabled: … && !touchInput`; no `complete_inline` request is ever made): the phone keyboard has its own suggestion strip, Korean IME composition keeps the draft "composing" so a ghost would shift every keystroke, and no clean accept gesture exists. The `nextPromptInline` switch still governs desktop.
  - Pinned by `InputArea.next-prompt-touch.test.tsx` (chip tap fills without sending, long press → Send now, first chip not the placeholder, no kbd/Tab hint, no `complete_inline`) and `e2e/mobile-suggestions.e2e.ts` (Pixel 8 emulation on the dev harness, which exposes `window.__stores.nextPrompt`; `OMP_GUI_SUGGEST_TOUCH=<dir>` writes the en/ko light captures).

### Mojibake from the model
- v0.33.0 showed UTF-8 read as Windows-1252 ("ì•Œê² ì–´ …" for "알겠어 …") in the placeholder and a chip. Nothing in OMPdesk decodes that text (bridge socket, omp stdout and IPC are UTF-8 end to end); the model itself wrote those characters. So OMPdesk **refuses, never repairs** (a model rarely spells every byte: Windows-1252 has no character for 0x81, 0x8D, 0x8F, 0x90, 0x9D; a no-break space 0xA0 becomes a plain space once whitespace is folded).
- `isUtf8Mojibake` (`resources/gui-bridge/mojibake.ts`, dependency-free) flags text with two or more whole UTF-8 sequences spelled in Windows-1252 characters (three- or four-byte ones, or Latin-1 letters led by Â/Ã). Real Latin-script text never forms them ("„Straße“", "café…", "ação" stay clean; pinned by `src/main/bridge-mojibake.test.ts`).
- Where applied: `cleanCandidateLine` drops the line; `cleanInlineReply` shows no ghost; automatic titles keep the current name (`concise` in `auto-title-engine.ts`, the shorter retry too); the idle recap is neither shown nor journaled (`session-recap-core.ts`, checked before `deliver`).

### Automatic session titles
- Built in, Settings › Agent behavior › Session titles. Prefs `autoTitle` and sub-switch `autoTitleEvolve` ("keep the title up to date as work changes"), both default **on**. Files `auto-title-contracts.ts`, `auto-title-core.ts`, `auto-title-engine.ts` behind `auto-title.ts`; capability `autoTitle`. Plumbing as prompt suggestions: `OMP_GUI_AUTO_TITLE` JSON at spawn, `auto_title_set` on change and on bridge connect to every live local sidecar and hosted tab; a paired host's tab follows the host's pref.
- **Stock machinery only, never the session model for a first title**: `AgentSession.generateTitle` (stock's `generateSessionTitle`, `tiny` → `commit` → `smol` role chain; the session model only as stock's own retry fallback after those failed) names the operator's first message (`operatorTitleText`); `renameTitle(title)` adds the card per `title.icons` (OMPdesk holds `boring`: no card, no card request — see Held stock defaults). A message the title model declines (a greeting) is retried from the conversation after the turn with `renameTitle()`. Main agent only; subagents are excluded.
- **Origin is stock's own record**: `SessionManager.titleSource` and the journal's `title_change` entries (`source`, `trigger`). The one case stock cannot say, "a fresh automatic title over a typed one" (`setSessionName(…,"auto")` is refused over source `user`), is written as source `user` with `trigger: "ompdesk-auto"` (`TITLE_TRIGGER`) and read back by `titleIsAuto` (`auto-title-core.ts`). Nothing lives in a GUI-only file.
- **Language, length, form**: stock's `title-system.md` is English-only and asks for 3-6 imperative words, so the model answered English and long. The engine sets the session's TITLE_SYSTEM.md slot (`setTitleSystemPrompt`, read by `generateTitle`, `renameTitle` and stock's replan refresh; the card request passes its own prompt) to stock's own `title-system.md` (`ompPrompt` + `prompt.render`, never copied) plus one line (`titleLanguageLine`): the language the user writes in first (for a non-English reply language, `currentReplyLanguage()`, that language when unclear); a plain noun-phrase summary, 2–4 words (`TITLE_MAX_WORDS`), ≤ 12 chars in Korean/Chinese/Japanese (`TITLE_MAX_CJK_CHARS`) and 30 otherwise (`TITLE_MAX_CHARS`), spaces included; subject and head noun kept, modifiers and generic words dropped; two `COMPRESSIONS` examples ("로그인 비밀번호 에러 메시지 버그 수정" → "로그인 오류 수정", "CSV export empty with filters" → "Empty CSV export"); no punctuation, quotes or emoji; identifiers and names as written. Set at attach, on `reply_language_set` (`onReplyLanguageChange`) and again before each request (stock's headless `/move` re-resolves the slot). Reply language off/unset/English → same line without the language fallback (the title stays in the user's language).
- **Over the limits** (`titleFits`), while the line is in effect: ONE retry, never more, never on the first pass, on a stronger model than the cheap chain (the cheap chain answered its own retry with the same 13–16-char titles): stock's `default` role (`roleCandidatePool` + `resolveRoleChain`), else the session's model, the first with credentials (`modelRegistry.getApiKey`/`resolver`), via stock `completeSimple`, reasoning off, `RETRY_MAX_TOKENS` 256, temperature 0, `cacheRetention: "none"` (Anthropic's 1 h cache write doubled a one-off request's cost).
  - System prompt = the slot's prompt + `titleShorterLine` (the too-long answer named, ≤ 12/30 chars, subject and head noun kept, modifiers and generic tails such as 화면, 메시지, 로직, 기능, 관련, "with …" dropped, all four `COMPRESSIONS` as examples); message = the operator's first message, or the answer itself when stock built the context for `renameTitle()` (✨, evolving). The reply's `<title>` goes through stock `normalizeGeneratedTitle`; counted in `requests`; a failed retry keeps the first answer.
  - The retry wins unless longer than the first answer (a tie goes to the retry) and is kept whole up to the hard cap (`TITLE_HARD_CJK_CHARS` 14, `TITLE_HARD_CHARS` 32, any word count). Past it `conciseTitle` cuts whole words, NEVER an ellipsis: in Korean/Chinese/Japanese generic words go first (`CJK_FILLER`: 화면, 메시지, 로직, 기능, 관련, 부분), then the leading words (the head noun comes last: a cut from the end turned "작업 현황판 화면 디자인" into "작업 현황판 화면"); otherwise the trailing ones, never ending on an English function word such as "with" or "the".
  - Every generated title is logged in omp's own log (`auto-title: title {answer, retry, retryModel, retryUsage, title}`). Cost reference (16 Oct 2026): a retry ≈ $0.005 (≈ 1.2 k input, 15–18 output tokens).
- Cards: under `title.icons: boring` a fresh title drops a card (stock's retitle keeps the current title's card through `keepTitleCard`, so ✨ and evolving on an old carded title come back plain; `cfgTitleIcons`, stock `splitCardTitle`); under another value the card is kept (`formatCardTitle`). Stored titles are never rewritten on their own. A person's own TITLE_SYSTEM.md (any slot value the engine did not set) is their whole title prompt: stays as written, uncut, never retried (`planTitlePrompt`). Setting the slot turns off stock's `title.generator: fork` path, which a GUI session never reaches (`autoTitle` is TUI-only in `main.ts`).
- **Evolving** (sub-switch): after a terminal `agent_end` while the title is automatic, `EvolveGate` (no model call; `DEFAULT_GATE_RULES`: `minTurns` 2 terminal turns and `minIntervalMs` 60 s since the title was set or last considered, and the work moved — a file edited/written 1, the todo plan changed 2, the goal changed 3, `threshold` 3 — or `forceTurns` 6 regardless) asks stock `renameTitle()` once per window. The result is applied only when not a reword of the current title (`isRewordedTitle`: normalized word overlap ≥ 0.6 `SAME_WORDS` or character-trigram overlap ≥ 0.55 `SAME_TRIGRAMS`). Every write re-checks the session id, the name and `titleRevision` it started from, so **a typed name wins over any request in flight and ends the evolving**.
- Cost: 1 title-model request for the first title (+1 for its card when `title.icons` ≠ `boring`, +1 retry on `default` when over the limits), then at most 1 per two finished turns and minute (+ its retry), only after files/plan/goal moved or six quiet turns (counted in `auto_title_set`'s `requests`).
- ✨ auto button (title bar, rename dialog, sidebar row; `hooks/use-auto-title.ts`, `common/AutoTitleButton.tsx`) sends `regenerate_session_title` → local handler → bridge `regenerate_title` (`renameTitle()` on the live session, saved as an automatic title even over a typed name; failure is one inline `role="alert"` sentence, never a toast). A stored session no tab has open has no omp to ask (origin tracking lives in that omp), so its button is aria-disabled with a tooltip. A paired host's tab answers on the host (the command is in `quota-commands.ts`' model list). A changed title eases in (`common/SwapText.tsx`, `.omp-title-swap`; the global reduced-motion rule shortens it).
- Tests: `bridge-auto-title-stock.test.ts` (against stock), `bridge-auto-title-core.test.ts`, `bridge-auto-title.test.ts`, `use-auto-title.test.ts`; `e2e/auto-title.e2e.ts` on the fake omp (`OMP_GUI_TEST_AUTO_TITLE`); opt-in `OMP_GUI_REAL_WORK=1` `e2e/real-auto-title.e2e.ts` (raw answers, retry model and usage into `/tmp/real-auto-title.json`, read from the private profile's omp log; `realOmpProfile(…, { keepDefaultRole: true })` keeps the user's `default` role and the audit moves its session to `smol` with stock `set_model`, which does not persist) checks four Korean first messages (Korean reply language) and two English ones (reply language off) get short plain titles in their own language, no card; four are the prompt's own compression examples, two held out.

### Automatic mode choice
- Settings › Agent behavior › Smart automation › Auto mode, and the "Auto mode" switch in the composer's Mode popover. Pref `autoMode`, default **on**, only `false` turns it off. Files `auto-mode-contracts.ts`, `auto-mode-core.ts`, per-session memory `auto-mode-state.ts`, behaviour `modes/auto-mode.ts`; capability `autoMode`.
- Decides **only Plan or Goal, or none**, and **uses that mode for the one request it classified, never as the session's mode** (the user's call after it put a busy work session into Plan). Other manual modes are never enabled by it.
- Classifier: stock has none (`plan.defaultOnStartup` is unconditional; `auto-thinking/classifier.ts` picks an effort), so the bridge copies its approach: stock `judgment` (`resolveJudge` over the `judge` role chain `@tiny` → `@smol` → `@default`; one choice question answered in a few tokens, `sharedJudgmentCache`, `journalJudgmentUsage` with purpose `auto-mode` so session totals count it) and `preprocessTinyMessage`. The judge's state carries `previousExchange` (last user message and reply, 400 chars each); its instructions say none for anything that continues, answers or corrects that work.
- No env or sync plumbing: the composer reads the pref at send time (`lib/auto-mode-client.ts` `runAutoMode`, called from `planComposerSubmit`'s `beforePrompt` for a plain prompt only, never steer/follow-up/slash) and calls `auto_mode_decide` (a model command in `remote/quota-commands.ts`; a paired host's tab runs it on the host, with the host's quota). The bridge method decides and changes nothing; main (`local/auto-mode.ts`) applies it through stock's own commands: bridge `modes.set_plan_mode` with `auto: true` for Plan (`auto-mode-state.ts` `autoPlan` marks it), stock `goal create` with the prompt text as objective for Goal.
- After a Goal decision `planComposerSubmit` sends the prompt with `streamingBehavior: "steer"`: on an idle session stock's `goal create` admits the goal's own turn at once (`rpc-goal.ts` `#scheduleContinuation`), ahead of the prompt, and stock refuses a plain prompt while a turn runs (`AgentBusyError`; sent plain it came back as "Message not sent"); steer joins that turn or starts one.
- **One request only**: an automatic plan ends when its plan is approved (stock's approval exits plan mode), rejected without feedback (`plan.ts` `approve` exits plan mode when `autoPlan` and answers `planModeEnabled: false`, which `PlanApprovalDialog` applies; feedback is a reply and re-plans), or the user sends a plain prompt while no proposal awaits approval (`runAutoMode` first sends `auto_mode_end` → main sends `modes.set_plan_mode {enabled: false}`). An automatic goal ends when it completes (`goal_updated`) or the user stops the turn (`abortActiveTurn` → `endAutoGoal` → `auto_mode_end` → stock `goal drop`). Neither ending is an undo: later prompts are still classified.
- **The Mode picker never shows it** (`ComposerModes` leaves `autoMode`'s plan/goal out of its label, badges and Plan switch); only the "This request: Plan/Goal" chip (`AutoModeChip`, store field `autoMode`, clears itself when the mode ends) does, with the undo. Turning Plan on in the picker over an automatic plan makes it the user's (`set_plan_mode` without `auto` clears `autoPlan`; the store's `autoMode` goes).
- **No hijacking busy sessions**: `autoModeSkip` answers `busy` without a request when the session ran anything within `AUTO_MODE_IDLE_MS` (10 min; the last message's timestamp), has open todos (pending/in progress) or running subagents/background jobs (`getAsyncJobSnapshot`) — so Plan only happens on a session's first message or after 10 idle minutes. Plan needs a stated probability ≥ `MIN_PLAN_PROBABILITY` 0.75, Goal ≥ `MIN_PROBABILITY` 0.6.
- Bounds: `AUTO_MODE_TIMEOUT_MS` 2000 ms in the bridge (3000 ms in the renderer; raised from 1500 because a cold first request or loaded machine often exceeded it and decided none); a timeout, failure, low probability or unknown answer is none; a message under `MIN_CLASSIFIED_CHARS` 12 is none without a request.
- Other guards: pref off, a turn running, `/` and `!` messages, any active mode the user picked (plan or goal: **a manual mode always wins**), and per session (`auto-mode-state.ts`, keyed by transcript id) a rejected plan (no plan again, goal still allowed) and an undone decision (`auto_mode_undo`, a free command: leaves the mode, stops all auto decisions for that session). Logged at debug level (`gui-bridge: auto mode`). Cost reference (16 Oct 2026, haiku-class `smol`/`tiny`): one judge request per eligible prompt, ≈ 430 input / 4 output tokens, ≈ $0.00005, 0.7–1.4 s.
- Tests: `bridge-auto-mode-core.test.ts` (guards incl. busy, plan threshold, memory), `bridge-auto-mode-stock.test.ts`, `auto-mode-client.test.ts` (plan ended by an unrelated message, kept for a reply, goal ended by Stop, manual plan untouched), `ComposerModes.auto-mode.test.tsx` (picker unchanged, manual takeover), `e2e/auto-mode.e2e.ts` (fake omp: `OMP_GUI_TEST_AUTO_MODE_IDLE_MS` sets its busy window, `OMP_GUI_TEST_AUTO_MODE_DELAY_MS` holds each decision like the real judge, `OMP_GUI_TEST_GOAL_TURN` `{holdMs, complete}` starts stock's goal turn on `goal create`, refuses a plain prompt during it and can complete the goal; covers plan ended by approval/rejection/unrelated message, goal ended by completion and Stop, a busy session left alone, a Mode popover with only the Plan and Goal tabs), opt-in real-model audit `bridge-auto-mode-real.test.ts` (`OMP_GUI_REAL_WORK=1`).

## Autopilot

### Overview
- UI **Autopilot** (ko "오토파일럿"; code id `autopilot`; Mode popover tab beside Plan and Goal): an endless development run in its own new git worktree tab, built on **stock Goal continuation** (`goal.continuationModes ∋ rpc`), no scheduler of its own. States `running | stopping | paused(reason) | waiting_quota | ended`; "End" = stock `goal pause` (turn in progress finishes) then drop; "Stop now" = `abort`.
- Contracts (journal entries, states, settings, `AutopilotStatus` carried by `get_autopilot` and the `autopilot_update` frame, `set_autopilot`'s `AutopilotAction`, `AUTOPILOT_BRIDGE_METHODS`) live in the dependency-free `resources/gui-bridge/modes/autopilot-contracts.ts`.
- **Bridge** (`resources/gui-bridge/modes/autopilot*.ts`): cycle ledger in the session journal, the `autopilot` tool, a static system section, and a `tool_call` guard (destructive git always blocked, no push/tag/publish for the agent, path checks, `eval`/`debug`/`ask` off the active tools).
- **Main** (`src/main/autopilot*.ts`): start, end/abort, children, approver, post-cycle git audit (second defence), `ShipRunner` (push/PR/merge/revert), release detection, restart resume.
- **Renderer**: store `stores/autopilot.ts`; screen `components/panels/autopilot/` (side-panel view `autopilot`, a `PanelView` outside `PANEL_TABS` like `subagent`, listed in View options only for an Autopilot tab, opened once per run when it starts by `lib/panel-auto-open.ts`, focused tab only, yielding to unsaved Files edits and a view the user works in); work board card (first section); composer chip "Autopilot · cycle N · area" with "open screen"; `GoalDockBar` strip; pure rules in `autopilot-view.ts`; log Markdown in `src/shared/autopilot-log.ts` (screen copy and draft PR body).
- An Autopilot tab's approval card is held back 3 s (`APPROVAL_GRACE_MS`; main's approver answers it). A rate-limit stop books `useQuotaResumeStore.reserve` itself (`lib/autopilot-quota.ts`, `QUOTA_FALLBACK_MS` 15 min when no report names a reset).
- Prefs (Settings › Agent behavior › Smart automation), validated by `validAutopilotPref`: `autopilotShip` (absent = Automatic, else a local/push/release policy), `autopilotNotify` (`stops | cycles | off`), `autopilotBranch`. There is no `autopilotLimits` pref.

### Tests
- Unit: `src/main/bridge-autopilot-*.test.ts`, `src/main/autopilot*.test.ts`, renderer `autopilot*` tests. Fake-omp e2e: `e2e/autopilot-{cycles,ship,restart}.e2e.ts` on `e2e/autopilot-harness.ts` (en/ko × light/dark captures, Stop versus ■, approvals, quota wait, guide, shipping against a temp repo with a local bare remote and a fake `gh`, SIGKILL relaunch and normal quit). Opt-in real omp: `e2e/real-autopilot.e2e.ts` (`OMP_GUI_REAL_WORK=1`: two cycles in a new worktree, the guard blocking a banned command, Stop, remote unchanged while local only, one push-level cycle; the yolo/tool-set test; the guide-draft test).
- Fake omp `e2e/fixture/autopilot.ts` (hooked into `rpc-ui.ts`; env `OMP_GUI_TEST_AUTOPILOT`, JSON keys documented at its top) emulates `modes.autopilot_*` and the frames by **importing** the bridge's own pure rules (`autopilot-core.ts`, `autopilot-guard-core.ts`, `restart-core.ts` `goalActiveAtExit`) over a journal written to the fake session file, and drives stock Goal continuation (create/pause/resume/drop as `mode_change` entries, abort pauses, `retry` re-arms, stdin EOF journals the turn aborted, a load pauses an active goal). Only the agent is scripted (tool calls through the real guard, real commits, real host tools for children, `end`); the yolo override is only recorded (`fixture_autopilot_session`), active tools are reported in `get_state.dumpTools`; it mirrors `pick` and first-end-stands (`endTwice`).

### Branch choice and target
- The start sheet's Branch row: a run commits on, and Push pushes to, the branch the user picks. `AutopilotStartOptions.branch` is `new` (default: `autopilot/<date>` made free of clashes, name editable, sent only when changed; `worktree_create` takes `branch: {name, mode: new|local|remote}` and never renames it) or `existing` (local, or only on the ship remote, checked out tracking `<remote>/<name>`). List = `AutopilotStatus.branches` (`src/main/autopilot-branches.ts`, re-read each time the sheet opens): default branch first (origin/HEAD, else main/master), then the folder's own, then the rest newest first, local+remote merged.
- **A branch another worktree holds is never refused or greyed**: it is the run's **TARGET**. The run works on its own `autopilot/<date>` cut from the target (`branch: {name, mode: "new", from}`, `viaWorkBranch` in `src/shared/autopilot-branch.ts`), journaled as `target` (arm params, state entry, `AutopilotStatus.target`, survives restart); `ShipRunner` pushes `<sha>:refs/heads/<target>` (`ShipTarget.pushTo`), fast-forward only. The row reads "via a work branch" with "작업 브랜치 → 푸시 대상" under it; the user's folder and working tree are never touched.
- **The default branch is allowed only when picked explicitly**, with a one-line warning ("Commits go straight to main; Push goes to main."/"커밋이 main에 바로 쌓이고, 푸시는 main에 해요"). A run committing on it is its own base (`baseBranch` = it, so a release's `HEAD:<default>` stays the one push the guard allows); Merge/PR refuse (`autopilot_on_base`).
- **A PR follows the push**: with a target, `ShipRunner.#pr` pushes the sha to the target (the only branch Autopilot ever pushes; the work branch never reaches the remote) and opens the PR from it. When that head is the base there is nothing to compare: Create PR is disabled with `autopilot.ship.prOnBase` and `set_autopilot ship pr` refuses with `autopilot_on_base` (Merge refuses only when the run's own branch is the base).
- Unchanged on any branch: the agent's pushes stay blocked (`ShipRunner` pushes `<sha>:refs/heads/<branch>`, never `+`); destructive git (force push, `reset`, branch delete/move, rewriting) stays banned in the guard; a non-fast-forward push is refused by git, never forced, and is a note to the agent (`#tellAgent` → bridge `note`, handed over by the next `begin`: fetch, merge, never force; text `<remote>/<branch> has commits this run lacks (not a fast-forward); Autopilot never forces a push.`), not a stop. The post-cycle audit judges the chosen branch (`AuditScope.branch`; the `autopilot/` prefix earns nothing). Run id = part after `autopilot/`, else the date.
- The choice persists in the journal as the arm's `branch` (`#adopt` reads `status.branch`). It is not part of `autopilotShip`; the pref `autopilotBranch` (`{[repo top level]: choice}`, written after a start) only prefills the next sheet while still usable. Rules are pure in `src/shared/autopilot-branch.ts`, shared by sheet and main.
- Pinned by `src/shared/autopilot-branch.test.ts`, `autopilot-branches.test.ts`, `autopilot.test.ts` ("the branch a run is on", restart), `autopilot-audit.test.ts`, `autopilot-ship.test.ts` ("pr"), `bridge-autopilot-guard.test.ts`, `src/main/local/git/worktree.test.ts`, the Branch tests in `ComposerModes.autopilot.test.tsx`, `AutopilotPanel.test.tsx`, and `e2e/autopilot-ship.e2e.ts` (existing branch moves on the remote, main does not; target = main leaves the folder untouched).

### No soft stops
- **The run does not stop for soft reasons.** Red cycles, a failed check, a missing report, compaction churn, cycles without a commit, a rejected/unreachable push, a failed release run, plan mode, all todos blocked, a quiet turn, a model failing after TurnResume's retries are repaired by the next cycle. `decideAfterCycle` only reports ceilings a start set; `classifyStall` returns `nudge` (frame `autopilot_nudge`: main leaves plan mode and sends one follow-up "call `autopilot begin`", at most every 20 s, 60 s after a failed turn). `AutopilotStatus.redStreak` is the counter: from two red in a row the timeline shows one muted line "연속 빨강 N"/"N red in a row" (`data-autopilot-red-streak`).
- Pauses left: the user's Pause/Stop/End and ■, `ban_violation` (destructive git, always), ceilings an agent's start set (`budget`/`time`/`cycles`/`cost`; none by default and none in the UI), `app_closed` (Continue interrupted work off), `repo_gone` (worktree or git dir vanished). `agent_done` ends the run; a quota wait resumes itself at the reset.
- Defaults: fan-out `maxParallel: null` (the agent decides, no cap; the protocol says optional, `begin` hints: fan out only for multi-minute items), shipping Automatic (see Start sheet), release cadence = agent decides, notifications = stops, focus = all, remote = origin or the only remote.

### Start sheet
- `components/modes/AutopilotConfig.tsx`: visible are the guide/direction box (`GrowArea`, the repo's `AUTOPILOT.md` or a draft), the full-access warning line and Start. Everything else sits in ONE closed `SettingsAdvanced` ("고급"/"Advanced"): 내보내기/Shipping (Automatic / Commit / Push / Release; Automatic, the default, names what it will do: "pushes the run's own branch to origin" or "no remote: commits stay here"), 브랜치/Branch (with the "작업 브랜치 → 푸시 대상" line under it when the target is held elsewhere), focus areas and the backlog. The sheet sends only what the user chose: a Start on the closed sheet sends `{action: "start"}` (plus the guide) and main decides the rest. Nothing in the popover changes layout on blur, Advanced included.
- **Automatic shipping** (`#start` in `src/main/autopilot.ts`, when neither the start nor the stored `autopilotShip` names a level): push when the repository has a remote and the branch the run pushes is not the default branch, else local; never a release. Pushing is ShipRunner's fast-forward of the run's own branch, so it is safe with a protected main and with or without CI (a local-only default left a repo with `origin` with nothing pushed).
- **No caps or ceilings in the UI**: fan-out is `maxParallel: null` (no cap; a number still caps it and 0 turns fan-out off, which `ompdesk_autopilot`'s `advanced.parallel` can send), notifications are Settings' `autopilotNotify` (stops), the release cadence is the agent's, the remote is detected, and ceilings exist only as `limits` an agent's start may set (`advanced.limits`).
- Settings › Agent behavior › Smart automation shows two rows (`components/settings/AutopilotRows.tsx`): Shipping (Automatic = no stored pref) and notifications.
- Pinned by `ComposerModes.autopilot.test.tsx` ("sends only what the user chose", "shows only the guide and Start with Advanced closed", "changes no layout on blur"), `AutopilotRows.test.tsx` and `autopilot.test.ts` ("the ship level nobody chose"); captures with `OMP_GUI_AUTOPILOT_CAPTURES`.

### Permissions
- Held only while the run lives. Arm takes `ask`/`eval`/`debug` off, adds `autopilot`, holds `tools.approvalMode = yolo` as a runtime override on the session's settings (never written to config.yml), and journals what it took off plus which were `xd://` devices (`excludedTools`, `mountedTools`). Disarm clears the override and restores with stock `setActiveToolPresentation(names, mounted)`: `getEnabledToolNames` also lists devices, and `setActiveToolsByName` would bring a device taken off at arm (`debug`) back top level.
- Pinned by `src/main/bridge-autopilot-stock.test.ts` (tool presentation before arm = after disarm) and the real-omp yolo/tool-set test in `e2e/real-autopilot.e2e.ts` (while armed, after Stop, and across a SIGKILL relaunch where the run continues by itself, armed before it runs).

### Restart
- **The user's rule: a restart continues the run, always** (crash, SIGKILL, normal quit, omp restart). Only Settings › General › Continue interrupted work off leaves it paused `app_closed` with Resume.
- `#onReady` sees the journal say `running` and asks `TurnResume.relaunchContinuation`: with a launch marker the run waits for TurnResume's restore of the cut-off turn (`resumeGoal`; `goalSettled` `none` continues anyway, `cancelled` or the resuming row's Stop pauses); without one (kill between turns) it continues at once. A run `#start` is still arming is no restart.
- Order: stock `goal pause`, arm with `resume`, `goal resume` (`#continueAfterRestart`). Why: stock's startup reconcile reattaches the goal paused and remembers the tool set of that moment (ask, eval, debug, no `autopilot`); its `goal resume` puts that set back and its `goal pause` restores and forgets it (`RpcGoalController` `#previousTools`). Without the pause the run lost its tool set until the next turn's `ensureTools` and every later pause gave the startup set back.
- On omp 18.11.3 a killed/quitting OMPdesk closes omp's stdin and stock journals the cut turn aborted, with a `session_exit` (real SIGKILL) or without (stock test), so `goalPausedByExit` must not need one (see Interrupted work, quit and diagnostics).
- Pinned by `autopilot.test.ts` (restart), `bridge-autopilot-stock.test.ts` (third test: stdin EOF mid-turn on the real omp), `e2e/autopilot-restart.e2e.ts` (kill mid-cycle, normal quit, kill between turns, setting off), and the real-omp yolo/tool-set test.

### Project guide (`AUTOPILOT.md`)
- At a repo's root (`AUTOPILOT_GUIDE_*` and `AutopilotGuideInfo` in `autopilot-contracts.ts`; rules in `modes/autopilot-guide.ts` + `boundGuide` in `autopilot-core.ts`): project-wide direction shared across sessions and computers (roadmap, area priorities, no-touch paths, done criteria, taste, release cadence, ideas). OMPdesk's own is `AUTOPILOT.md` at this repo's root. It is **not** the run's backlog or notes (those stay in the session journal and return through `begin`); nothing ever writes app backlog items into it.
- Every `autopilot begin` reads it from the worktree root (`state.root`) and returns `begin.guide` (≤ 8 KB, `AUTOPILOT_GUIDE_MAX_BYTES`, cut on a character boundary with a marker; absent/blank leaves the field out), so an edit applies from the next cycle. The static system section stays byte-identical (says the guide exists, ranks below the rules files, never lifts the HARD LIMITS): rules files (AGENTS.md etc.) win over the guide.
- A repo without one gets a draft in the sheet (once per sheet): action `guide_draft` → bridge `modes.autopilot_guide {root, draft}` → ONE `completeOnSmol` request (`next-prompt-engine.ts`: stock's `smol` role via `resolveRoleChain`, never the main model, usage in stock's ledger as `autopilot-guide-draft`) over a bounded prompt (rules-file excerpts, README, recent commit subjects, test/build commands); on any failure the deterministic `templateGuide`. An existing guide is shown for editing (`get_autopilot` carries `guide` for a session that is no run; sheet cap `AUTOPILOT_GUIDE_SHEET_MAX_BYTES` 64 KB).
- Start sends `AutopilotStartOptions.guide`; `#commitGuide` writes it into the new worktree and commits it (`--no-verify`, no signing) as the branch's first commit when the file differs, so it never touches the user's current branch and reaches main only on merge.
- Pinned by `bridge-autopilot-guide.test.ts`, the sheet tests in `ComposerModes.autopilot.test.tsx`, the guide test of `e2e/autopilot-cycles.e2e.ts`.

### Cycle reports are checked, not trusted
- Why (real omp, small model: 11 of 11 cycles told the truth about their check, but the commit hash was missing in 5 of 7 reports and a second `end` overwrote the first). Pure rules in `modes/autopilot-verify.ts` (pinned by `bridge-autopilot-verify.test.ts`), wiring in `autopilot.ts`.
- **The first `end` of a turn stands**: a repeat only adds follow-ups, children, ship, commit; `shouldSteerStop` sends no stop steer once reported. Only a refused report is replaced by a second `end`.
- `observeReport` takes the commit from git (a claimed hash not in the branch history is dropped; no repo, the claim stands) and measures `change` (`AutopilotChangeStat`: files, +/−, commits, `docOnly`).
- **A green claim needs the turn's own tool calls to show it** (ledger of `tool_execution_*`: a check-like `bash` command, or the one named in `end.check`, that did not fail and ran after the last `edit`/`write`/`ast_edit`). Otherwise `verify` becomes `skipped` or `red` with `verifyNote` (`no_check` | `check_failed` | `edited_after`; timeline in en/ko and Markdown log); the `end` result tells the agent, which may run the check and `end` again. A cycle with a commit and no green counts red: neither pushed nor ignored. Release cycles are exempt.
- A `pick` op names the item after the audit (`now.title`; before it `now.area` is unset and the screen says "Picking an item…", never the rotation's guess) and refuses a repeat of a recent title before the work.
- `begin` carries `pick` and `hints` (a follow-up carried ≥ 3 cycles asks for a decision, a docs-only last cycle, fan-out cost); all dynamic guidance rides in `begin`, the static section is unchanged. Fan-out measured: two 25–30 s items took 62 s and cost about twice as much in two children, hence "fan out only for items of several minutes".
- **Bugs before polish** (19 Oct 2026; a real run spent 2 of 4 cycles on docs and wrote a found bug off as a "known limitation"): `BEGIN_PICK` ranks a known bug or failing behaviour first, docs and polish last, and says a fixable bug is never a limitation; `beginHints` names up to two open follow-ups whose words mark a defect (`DEFECT`); `pick` refuses a docs item right after a docs-only cycle unless it carries `reason` (`docsPickRefusal`); `end` answers a report that writes a problem off with no follow-up with `writeOff` (`writeOffNote`), and the repeated `end` merges the follow-up. Pure in `autopilot-core.ts`, pinned by `bridge-autopilot-verify.test.ts`. Real run after it: 3 bug cycles and 1 docs cycle of 4, both seeded bugs and a third one fixed, nothing written off.
- **The run's session title** (`runSessionTitle`, `#retitle` in `src/main/autopilot.ts`): stock `set_session_name` "오토파일럿 · <repo folder>" once the tab is ready, then "… · <cycle 1's item>" from `pick` (or cycle 1's record), only while the name is still the run's own (`get_state.sessionName`). Stock answers an RPC rename without `session_info_update`, so `SidecarPool.noteSessionName` tells the tab entry and the windows. Pinned by `autopilot.test.ts` ("titles the run's session").
- The run screen has no empty-state sentences: Areas, Backlog and Timeline show only their rows; Backlog's and Focus's explanations sit behind (i) (`AutopilotPanel.test.tsx` "one-line rows").

## OMPdesk control tools

### Overview and safety
- Switch: Settings › Tools & extensions › OMPdesk control tools, pref `agentControl`, default **on**. Contract `src/shared/agent-control.ts`, rules `src/main/agent-control/service.ts`, schemas `tools.ts`, app seam `host.ts`, skill `resources/omp-builtin/skills/ompdesk/SKILL.md`.
- **Full control** (the user's decision, 19 Oct 2026): Settings › Tools & extensions, first row "Give agents full control of OMPdesk" / "에이전트에게 OMPdesk 모든 권한", pref `agentControlFull` (`AGENT_CONTROL_FULL_PREF`), default **on** for every install (`agentControlFullEnabled`: only a stored `false` is off). While on, **no control tool or app action raises an `[OMPdesk]` card**: the `always` set (deletes, MCP/plugin install, sign-in starts, proxy, mobile web on, pairing, update apply, quit), a foreign session delete, Autopilot release or main targets, every power pref; it implies `agentControlApprove` and `agentControlManage` (`host.ts` resolves both as `full || stored`; the Settings rows show them checked and disabled as covered), and the catalog action `agent_power.set` (`fullOnly`; `FULL_CONTROL_PREFS`/`FULL_CONTROL_SETTINGS` in `app-guard.ts`, the two memory switches through ai-memory's own channels) lets an agent change the agent-power switches, this one included. Every action is still journaled and toasted with undo where one exists. Kept whatever it says: an agent never answers an `[OMPdesk]` card (and with the switch on none is raised), secrets/tokens/passwords are never readable, git safety (no force push, Autopilot's guard bans destructive git), and OS security prompts (UAC, TCC). Its tooltip names the risk: prompt-injected content can then make an agent delete data or install code unasked. **Every card rule below applies only while full control is off.** Pinned by `shared/agent-control.test.ts`, `app-control.test.ts` (each `always` action runs card-free when on, `agent_power.set` refused when off), `service.test.ts`, `host.test.ts`, `AgentControlRow.test.tsx`, and the on/off cases of `e2e/agent-app-control.e2e.ts`, `e2e/agent-control.e2e.ts`, `e2e/agent-review.e2e.ts`.
- Host tools (like `gui_terminal_read`) are registered with stock `set_host_tools` on every ready local sidecar (`SidecarManager.syncHostTools`, re-sent when the switch changes) and answered in `ipc.ts` (`hostToolExecutor` → `AgentControlRuntime.run`). The skill's root `resources/omp-builtin` is a `-e` directory only while the switch is on, so skill and tools come and go with the pref.
- Tools (`AGENT_CONTROL_TOOLS`): `ompdesk_sessions`, `_session_open` (one or `sessions: […]`, optional `worktree: true | {branch, base: HEAD|default}`), `_session_send` (steer or follow-up), `_session_result` (several ids, `wait`, `until all|any`), `_session_read` (pages via `get_transcript_page`), `_session_answer`, `ompdesk_tab_focus` (renderer pick/split via `AGENT_CONTROL_VIEW`), `ompdesk_notify`, `_session_stop` (`close`, `delete`), `ompdesk_compare`, `ompdesk_merge`, `ompdesk_schedule`, `ompdesk_autopilot`, `ompdesk_app_list`, `ompdesk_app`, `ompdesk_report`.
- Everything runs through existing GUI paths: prompts/steers through `dispatchRpcCommand` (a paired host's tab goes over the link, so the host's grants and the per-device quota apply; `src/main/remote/quota-commands.ts` lists `agent_control_confirm`/`agent_control_note` as free), `spawnTabForWindow` to open, the renderer's own close, `deleteSession` for delete.
- **Subagents have no control tools** (stock registers host tools on the RPC session only; `host_tool_call` carries no caller id). `e2e/real-agent-control.e2e.ts` checks it: a subagent answers `NO_TOOL` and no tab opens.
- **No count or rate caps** (the user's rule, 19 Oct 2026; none exist in `src/main/agent-control`): any number of sessions opened, messages sent, schedules and Autopilot runs. Only a self-send is refused. Provenance ("opened by you") is main-owned state, never read from text or tool args; it survives a relaunch (`#openedBy` adds every tab whose `host.parentOf` is the caller: the pool's `openedBy`, else the tab holding `openedBySession`).
- Session tools touch no accounts, settings or permissions. **Deleting a saved session the caller did not open** (`ompdesk_session_stop delete:true`) always raises one confirmation card (stock extension-UI `confirm` via the calling tab's omp, bridge `agent_control.confirm`, title prefix `[OMPdesk]`, so it survives a reload and reaches a paired device), whatever "Let agents manage other sessions" says: a delete cannot be undone. Stopping or closing a session it did not open raises the same card only while that switch (`agentControlManage`, **default on**: `agentControlManageEnabled` in `shared/agent-control.ts` treats an unset pref as on and only a stored `false` as off) is off. Sessions it opened itself need none, delete included.
- Closing a resting session's tab (not busy, no open ask, not in the person's view, no draft or prompt of theirs since: `ControlTab.looked`/`userTouched`) raises no card whoever opened it; a session in view or typed into is never closed (re-checked right before the close). Each close is journaled as one quiet `ompdesk.mail` note in both sessions.
- `_answer` answers asks (select, confirm, input, ask); the answered session journals `ompdesk.agent-answer` and toasts "에이전트가 답함 (title)" (bridge `agent_control.note`). A **tool approval** ("Allow tool: …") is approved only when "Let agents approve for other sessions" (`agentControlApprove`, **default off** on purpose: an agent approving another session's tool bypasses the user's own approval policy, so it stays the user's explicit choice; `agentControlApproveEnabled` is true only for a stored `true`) is on, else "사용자 승인이 필요해요" (deny always works). A card titled `[OMPdesk] …` is never answerable.
